# Lazarus Codex

Turn rough intent into bounded, verified work while keeping main context focused.

## Load order

1. Read `../../assets/lazarus/adapters/codex-runtime-pilot.md`. This is the Codex activation and optional CLI-review contract; it supersedes historical PAL-retirement and Claude-only routing text for this host's explicitly authorized pilot.

3. Load `../../assets/lazarus/workflow.md` and its selected detailed route only when the task needs the full scaffold. Do not load every registry for a direct answer or a small scoped edit.
4. For durable PDCA cycles, load `../../assets/lazarus/skills/pdca-cycle-advisor.md` with `pdca-loop.md`; resolve the helper as this plugin root's `scripts/pdca_cycle.py`.

# build — implement spec

Single-thread native plan→execute. You are main Claude. No swarm.

## LOAD

1. Read `SPEC.md`. If missing → tell user to invoke the spec skill first. Stop.
2. Read `FORMAT.md` once if not loaded.
3. Parse invocation args:
   - `§T.n` → that task only
   - `--next` → lowest-numbered row with status `.` or `~`
   - `--all` or empty → every `.` row in §T order

## PLAN

Native plan mode. For chosen task(s):

1. Cite every §V invariant that applies. Plan must respect all.
2. Cite every §I interface touched. Plan must preserve shape.
3. List files to create / edit.
4. List tests to add or update (one per invariant touched).
5. Name verification command (test, build, lint).

Show plan. Wait for user OK unless auto mode.

## EXECUTE

Per task in order:

1. Flip §T.n status cell `.` → `~`. Just write to SPEC.md.
2. Edit code per plan.
3. Run verification command.
4. **Pass** → flip `~` → `x`. Next task.
5. **Fail** → invoke backprop skill. Do NOT retry blindly.

## FAIL → BACKPROP

On test/build failure:

1. Read failure output.
2. Ask: is failure (a) my code bug, (b) spec wrong, or (c) unspecified edge case?
3. If (a) → fix code, re-run. No spec change.
4. If (b) or (c) → invoke spec skill with `bug: <cause>` first, let it update §V and §B, then resume build against updated spec.

Rule: never silently fix root-cause without considering backprop. §B is the memory that stops recurrence.

## WRITE POLICY

- Only flip §T status. No other SPEC.md edits from build.
- Other spec edits → invoke spec skill.
- Commit after each §T completes. Message: `T<n>: <goal line>` + §V cites.

## VERIFICATION

Task `x` only if:
- Verification command exits 0.
- New test(s) added per plan.
- No §V invariant regressed (run full test suite at end).

## NON-GOALS

- No sub-agents. No parallel workers. Main thread only.
- No progress dashboards. `cat SPEC.md | grep §T` is the dashboard.
- No speculative work beyond chosen task scope.

# Codex runtime and optional PAL pilot

Owner: Codex host adapter. Authorized 2026-09-14. This host-specific pilot supersedes historical "never route PAL" statements only for the separate `lazarus-pal-pilot` server. The legacy embedded PAL bundle remains retired; do not restore old API keys, retired shims, or old permissive CLI presets.

## Activation and work loop

Invoke the installed `lazarus` skill or ask "Use Lazarus". If the skill is absent, inspect plugin enabled state before guessing another invocation. A plugin reinstall requires a fresh thread to expose updated skills and tools.

1. Plan: state objective, scoped artifacts, assumptions, observable checks, and rollback for operational changes. Plan-only requests remain plan-only.
2. Do: execute authorized work. Main handles small scoped work and prompt-system self-modification. Delegate substantial implementation when it materially helps, with disjoint ownership and concrete checks.
3. Check: inspect actual artifacts and run proportionate checks. Advice and worker self-report never prove acceptance.
4. Act: integrate verified results, record material failures, and report remaining limits. Existing hooks remain active; do not bypass a rejection.

No mandatory external model review for every task. Do not load unrelated scaffold registries or start a second review only to satisfy an obsolete Claude-only sequence. Preserve durable cycle tracking when explicitly requested or already active.

The independently registered `lazarus-pal-pilot` MCP server exposes `review_plan`; discover its exact callable name in the current host before using it. It uses unmodified PAL 9.8.2 CLI execution through a small local entrypoint, not PAL's full API-provider server.

- `provider="agy"`: Gemini `gemini-3.8-flash-high`, effort high, mode plan, sandbox. Review only. Never combine plan mode with `--disable-slash-commands`, which disables the mode.
- `provider="codex"`: GPT-5.5/high advisory review in a read-only sandbox. `account="work"` or `"revolut"` selects an existing separately authenticated CLI home. This review role does not change the Spark implementation preference.
- Pass a self-contained plan, explicit working directory when needed, and a narrow review question. Default working directory is `/tmp`.

- PAL and AGY responses are advisory. Host retains final verification and workflow ownership.

CLI fallback, when the MCP tool is not yet exposed: pipe the supplied plan into `lazarus-review --provider agy`, or `lazarus-review --provider codex --account revolut`. Credentials remain managed by the CLIs.

Each launch selects a child process's existing account home; the main app account is unchanged. Authenticate with `lazarus-account revolut login` only if status or a live request requires it. - Existing behavior is preserved unless the user requested otherwise.
- Tests or equivalent checks were run, or the reason they could not run is reported.
- No unrelated files were reformatted or rewritten.

import type { URI } from '@foam/core';
import type { Resource, Connection } from '@foam/core';
import { buildGraphData } from '@foam/core';
import type { GraphData, NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
  'resolution-mode': 'import',
};
import { classifyRoots, makeResourceNodeId } from './roots';

export function buildRootQualifiedGraphData(
  resources: Resource[],
  connections: Connection[],
  workspaceRoots: URI[]
): { graphData: GraphData; idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI> } {
  const classification = classifyRoots(workspaceRoots);
  const builtGraphData = buildGraphData(resources, connections, {
    resourceToId: uri => makeResourceNodeId(uri, classification.roots),
    includePlaceholders: false,
  });

  const idToUri = new Map<NodeId, URI>();
  for (const resource of resources) {
    const id = makeResourceNodeId(resource.uri, classification.roots);
    if (id !== undefined) {
      idToUri.set(id, resource.uri);
    }
  }

  const graphData: GraphData = {
    ...builtGraphData,
    rootGroups: classification.rootGroups,
  };

  return { graphData, idToUri };
}
import type { URI } from '@foam/core';
import { relativeTo, isWithinPath } from '@foam/core';
// @ts-expect-error The graph-view package publishes its runtime protocol as ESM while this package type-checks as CommonJS.
import { makeNodeId, parseNodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol';
import type { NodeId, RootGroup, RootKind } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
  'resolution-mode': 'import',
};

export interface ClassifiedRoot {
  uri: URI;
  kind: RootKind;
}

export interface RootClassification {
  roots: ClassifiedRoot[];
  rootGroups: RootGroup[];
}

const ROOT_GROUPS: Record<RootKind, RootGroup> = {
  vault: { root: 'vault', label: 'Vault', color: '#4a9eff' },
  sources: { root: 'sources', label: 'Sources', color: '#ff9f43' },
};

export function classifyRoots(
  workspaceRoots: URI[],
  classification?: Map<URI, RootKind>
): RootClassification {
  const roots = workspaceRoots.map((uri, index): ClassifiedRoot => {
    const mappedKind =
      classification?.get(uri) ??
      [...(classification?.entries() ?? [])].find(([classifiedUri]) =>
        classifiedUri.isEqual(uri)
      )?.[1];
    return {
      uri,
      kind: mappedKind ?? (index === 0 ? 'vault' : 'sources'),
    };
  });

  const rootGroups = Array.from(new Set(roots.map(root => root.kind))).map(
    kind => ({ ...ROOT_GROUPS[kind] })
  );

  return { roots, rootGroups };
}

export function resolveRoot(
  uri: URI,
  classifiedRoots: ClassifiedRoot[]
): ClassifiedRoot | undefined {
  return classifiedRoots.find(root => isWithinPath(uri, root.uri));
}

function isValidNodePath(path: string): boolean {
  return parseNodeId(`vault:${path}`)?.path === path;
}

export function makeResourceNodeId(
  uri: URI,
  classifiedRoots: ClassifiedRoot[]
): NodeId | undefined {
  const root = resolveRoot(uri, classifiedRoots);
  if (!root) {
    return undefined;
  }

  const relativePath = relativeTo(uri.path, root.uri.path);
  if (!isValidNodePath(relativePath)) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return makeNodeId(root.kind, relativePath);
}
import {
  Memento,
  Position,
  Range,
  Uri,
  TextEdit,
  WorkspaceEdit,
  commands,
  Diagnostic,
  DiagnosticSeverity,
  DiagnosticRelatedInformation,
  Location,
} from 'vscode';
import { Position as FoamPosition } from '@foam/core';
import { Range as FoamRange } from '@foam/core';
import { URI as FoamURI, fromFsPath } from '@foam/core';
import {
  TextEdit as FoamTextEdit,
  WorkspaceTextEdit,
} from '@foam/core';
import { LintIssue } from '@foam/core';
import { FoamWorkspace } from '@foam/core';
import { Logger } from '@foam/core';

export const toVsCodePosition = (p: FoamPosition): Position =>
  new Position(p.line, p.character);

export const toVsCodeRange = (r: FoamRange): Range =>
  new Range(r.start.line, r.start.character, r.end.line, r.end.character);

export const toVsCodeUri = (u: FoamURI): Uri => Uri.from(u);

export const fromVsCodeUri = (u: Uri): FoamURI =>
  new FoamURI({
    scheme: u.scheme,
    authority: u.authority,
    path: fromFsPath(u.path)[0],
    query: u.query,
    fragment: u.fragment,
  });

export const toVsCodeTextEdit = (edit: FoamTextEdit): TextEdit =>
  new TextEdit(toVsCodeRange(edit.range), edit.newText);

/**
 * Convert WorkspaceTextEdit array to VS Code WorkspaceEdit.
 *
 * @param workspaceTextEdits Array of workspace text edits to convert
 * @param workspace Foam workspace for URI resolution
 * @returns VS Code WorkspaceEdit ready for application
 */
export const toVsCodeWorkspaceEdit = (
  workspaceTextEdits: WorkspaceTextEdit[],
  workspace: FoamWorkspace
): WorkspaceEdit => {
  const workspaceEdit = new WorkspaceEdit();

  // Group edits by URI
  const editsByUri = new Map<string, { uri: Uri; edits: TextEdit[] }>();

  for (const workspaceTextEdit of workspaceTextEdits) {
    const resource = workspace.get(workspaceTextEdit.uri);
    if (!resource) {
      Logger.warn(
        `Could not resolve resource: ${workspaceTextEdit.uri.toString()}`
      );
      continue;
    }

    const vscodeUri = toVsCodeUri(resource.uri);
    const uriKey = resource.uri.toString();
    const existingEntry = editsByUri.get(uriKey) || {
      uri: vscodeUri,
      edits: [],
    };

    const vscodeEdit = new TextEdit(
      toVsCodeRange(workspaceTextEdit.edit.range),
      workspaceTextEdit.edit.newText
    );

    existingEntry.edits.push(vscodeEdit);
    editsByUri.set(uriKey, existingEntry);
  }

  // Apply grouped edits to workspace
  for (const { uri, edits } of editsByUri.values()) {
    workspaceEdit.set(uri, edits);
  }

  return workspaceEdit;
};

/**
 * Converts a platform-agnostic LintIssue to a VS Code Diagnostic.
 * Severity is always Warning; source is always 'Foam'.
 * relatedInfo entries are mapped directly using their message field.
 */
export const lintIssueToDiagnostic = (issue: LintIssue): Diagnostic => {
  const diagnostic = new Diagnostic(
    toVsCodeRange(issue.range),
    issue.message,
    DiagnosticSeverity.Warning
  );
  diagnostic.code = issue.code;
  diagnostic.source = 'Foam';
  if (issue.relatedInfo) {
    diagnostic.relatedInformation = issue.relatedInfo.map(
      info =>
        new DiagnosticRelatedInformation(
          new Location(toVsCodeUri(info.uri), toVsCodeRange(info.range)),
          info.message
        )
    );
  }
  return diagnostic;
};

/**
 * A class that wraps context value, syncs it via setContext, and provides a typed interface to it.
 */
export class ContextMemento<T> {
  private defaultValue: T;
  constructor(
    private data: Memento,
    private key: string,
    defaultValue: T,
    resetToDefault: boolean = false
  ) {
    this.defaultValue = defaultValue;
    resetToDefault && this.data.update(this.key, defaultValue);
    const value = data.get(key) ?? defaultValue;
    commands.executeCommand('setContext', this.key, value);
  }
  public get(): T {
    return this.data.get<T>(this.key) ?? this.defaultValue;
  }
  public async update(value: T): Promise<void> {
    this.data.update(this.key, value);
    await commands.executeCommand('setContext', this.key, value);
  }
}

/**
 * Implementation of the Memento interface that uses a Map as backend
 */
export class MapBasedMemento implements Memento {
  get<T>(key: unknown, defaultValue?: unknown | T): T | T {
    return (this.map.get(key as string) as T) || (defaultValue as T);
  }
  private map: Map<string, string> = new Map();
  keys(): readonly string[] {
    return Array.from(this.map.keys());
  }
  update(key: string, value: any): Promise<void> {
    this.map.set(key, value);
    return Promise.resolve();
  }
}
import * as vscode from 'vscode';
import { Foam, URI } from '@foam/core';
import { Logger } from '@foam/core';
import { fromVsCodeUri } from '../../utils/vsc-utils';
import { isSome } from '@foam/core';
import { getFoamVsCodeConfig } from '../../config';
import { getActiveTabUri, onDidChangeActiveTab } from '../../services/editor';
import { buildGraphData } from '@foam/core';
import {
  buildCsp,
  fillGraphHtml,
  generateNonce,
} from '../../../pa-hub/webview-security';
import type {
  GraphStyle,
  GraphViewConfig,
  ShowGraphArgs,
} from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with { 'resolution-mode': 'import' };

interface GraphWebviewHost {
  webview: vscode.Webview;
}

class GraphWebviewProvider implements vscode.WebviewViewProvider {
  private webviewView: vscode.WebviewView | undefined;

  constructor(
    private readonly context: vscode.ExtensionContext,
    private readonly foamPromise: Promise<Foam>
  ) {}

  async resolveWebviewView(
    webviewView: vscode.WebviewView,
    _context: vscode.WebviewViewResolveContext,
    _token: vscode.CancellationToken
  ): Promise<void> {
    this.webviewView = webviewView;

    const datavizUri = vscode.Uri.joinPath(
      this.context.extensionUri,
      'static',
      'dataviz'
    );
    webviewView.webview.options = {
      enableScripts: true,
      localResourceRoots: [datavizUri],
    };

    const nonce = generateNonce();
    const csp = buildCsp({
      cspSource: webviewView.webview.cspSource,
      nonce,
    });
    const template = new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(
      await vscode.workspace.fs.readFile(
        vscode.Uri.joinPath(datavizUri, 'index.html')
      )
    );
    const filled = fillGraphHtml(template, {
      csp,
      nonce,
      resolveAsset: fileName =>
        webviewView.webview
          .asWebviewUri(vscode.Uri.joinPath(datavizUri, fileName))
          .toString(),
    });
    webviewView.webview.html = filled;

    const foam = await this.foamPromise;
    webviewView.webview.onDidReceiveMessage(
      async message => {
        switch (message.type) {
          case 'webviewDidLoad': {
            const style = getGraphStyle();
            webviewView.webview.postMessage({
              type: 'didUpdateStyle',
              payload: style,
            });

            updateGraph(webviewView, foam);
            break;
          }
          case 'webviewDidSelectNode': {
            const noteUri = vscode.Uri.parse(message.payload);
            const selectedNote = foam.workspace.get(fromVsCodeUri(noteUri));

            if (isSome(selectedNote)) {
              const navigateToPreview = getFoamVsCodeConfig(
                'graph.navigateToPreview',
                false
              );
              const command = getNodeNavigationCommand(
                noteUri.path,
                navigateToPreview
              );
              if (command === 'markdown.showPreview') {
                vscode.commands.executeCommand(command, noteUri);
              } else {
                vscode.commands.executeCommand(
                  command,
                  noteUri,
                  vscode.ViewColumn.One
                );
              }
            }
            break;
          }
          case 'webviewDidBecomeReady': {
            break;
          }
          case 'error': {
            Logger.error('An error occurred in the graph view', message.payload);
            break;
          }
        }
      },
      undefined,
      this.context.subscriptions
    );

    const noteUpdatedListener = foam.graph.onDidUpdate(() => {
      updateGraph(webviewView, foam);
    });
    const editorListener = onDidChangeActiveTab(() => {
      handleActiveResourceChange(
        webviewView,
        foam,
        getActiveTabUri(foam.workspace)
      );
    });
    webviewView.onDidDispose(() => {
      noteUpdatedListener.dispose();
      editorListener.dispose();
      if (this.webviewView === webviewView) {
        this.webviewView = undefined;
      }
    });
  }

  updateStyle(style: GraphStyle): void {
    this.webviewView?.webview.postMessage({
      type: 'didUpdateStyle',
      payload: style,
    });
  }
}

export default async function activate(
  context: vscode.ExtensionContext,
  foamPromise: Promise<Foam>
) {
  const provider = new GraphWebviewProvider(context, foamPromise);
  context.subscriptions.push(
    vscode.workspace.onDidChangeConfiguration(event => {
      if (event.affectsConfiguration('pa-hub.graph.style')) {
        provider.updateStyle(getGraphStyle());
      }
    }),
    vscode.window.registerWebviewViewProvider('pa-hub.graph', provider, {
      webviewOptions: { retainContextWhenHidden: true },
    })
  );

  context.subscriptions.push(
    vscode.commands.registerCommand(
      'pa-hub.show-graph',
      () => vscode.commands.executeCommand('pa-hub.graph.focus')
    )
  );
}

function updateGraph(webviewHost: GraphWebviewHost, foam: Foam) {
  const graph = generateGraphData(foam);
  webviewHost.webview.postMessage({
    type: 'didUpdateGraphData',
    payload: graph,
  });
}

function generateGraphData(foam: Foam) {
  return buildGraphData(foam.workspace.list(), foam.graph.getAllConnections(), {
    resourceToId: uri => uri.path,
    transformTitle: title => cutTitle(title),
    includePlaceholders: true,
  });
}

export function cutTitle(title: string): string {
  const maxLen = vscode.workspace
    .getConfiguration('pa-hub.graph')
    .get('titleMaxLength', 24);
  if (maxLen > 0 && title.length > maxLen) {
    return title.substring(0, maxLen).concat('...');
  }
  return title;
}

export function getGraphStyle(): GraphStyle {
  const styleConfig =
    vscode.workspace.getConfiguration('pa-hub.graph').get('style') ?? {};
  return { style: styleConfig };
}

export function viewConfigToStyle(config: GraphViewConfig): GraphStyle {
  const nodeColors = config.show
    ? Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(config.show)
          .filter(([, cfg]) => cfg.color)
          .map(([type, cfg]) => [type, cfg.color!])
      )
    : undefined;

  const showNodesOfType = config.show
    ? Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(config.show).map(([type, cfg]) => [
          type,
          cfg.enabled ?? true,
        ])
      )
    : undefined;

  const styleProps: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (config.background !== undefined)
    styleProps.background = config.background;
  if (config.fontSize !== undefined) styleProps.fontSize = config.fontSize;
  if (config.fontFamily !== undefined)
    styleProps.fontFamily = config.fontFamily;
  if (config.lineColor !== undefined) styleProps.lineColor = config.lineColor;
  if (nodeColors && Object.keys(nodeColors).length > 0)
    styleProps.node = nodeColors;

  return {
    ...(config.colorBy !== undefined ? { colorMode: config.colorBy } : {}),
    ...(config.groups !== undefined ? { groups: config.groups } : {}),
    ...(showNodesOfType !== undefined ? { showNodesOfType } : {}),
    ...(Object.keys(styleProps).length > 0 ? { style: styleProps } : {}),
  };
}

export function mergeStyles(base: GraphStyle, patch: GraphStyle): GraphStyle {
  return {
    colorMode: patch.colorMode ?? base.colorMode,
    groups: patch.groups ?? base.groups,
    showNodesOfType: { ...base.showNodesOfType, ...patch.showNodesOfType },
    style: { ...base.style, ...patch.style },
  };
}

export function resolveViewStyle(args?: ShowGraphArgs): {
  style: GraphStyle;
  view?: string;
} {
  const views: GraphViewConfig[] =
    vscode.workspace.getConfiguration('pa-hub.graph').get('views') ?? [];

  let style: GraphStyle = getGraphStyle();

  // If no view name given and no inline config, fall back to the "Default" named view
  const namedView = args?.view ?? (args?.config ? undefined : 'Default');

  if (namedView) {
    const view = views.find(v => v.name === namedView);
    if (view) {
      style = mergeStyles(style, viewConfigToStyle(view));
    }
  }

  if (args?.config) {
    style = mergeStyles(style, viewConfigToStyle(args.config));
  }

  return { style, view: args?.view };
}

export function handleActiveResourceChange(
  webviewHost: GraphWebviewHost | undefined,
  foam: Foam,
  uri: URI | undefined
) {
  if (webviewHost && uri && uri.scheme !== 'untitled') {
    const note = foam.workspace.find(uri);
    if (isSome(note)) {
      webviewHost.webview.postMessage({
        type: 'didSelectNote',
        payload: note.uri.path,
      });
    }
  }
}

export function getNodeNavigationCommand(
  uriPath: string,
  navigateToPreview: boolean
): string {
  if (navigateToPreview && uriPath.endsWith('.md')) {
    return 'markdown.showPreview';
  }
  return 'vscode.open';
}
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import * as vscode from 'vscode';
import { URI } from '@foam/core';
import {
  cutTitle,
  getGraphStyle,
  getNodeNavigationCommand,
  handleActiveResourceChange,
  mergeStyles,
  resolveViewStyle,
  viewConfigToStyle,
} from '../../vscode/features/graph-webview/index';

beforeEach(async () => {
  const config = vscode.workspace.getConfiguration();
  await config.update('pa-hub.graph.style', {});
  await config.update('pa-hub.graph.titleMaxLength', 24);
  await config.update('pa-hub.graph.views', []);
});

describe('graph view helpers', () => {
  it('test_graph_view_get_node_nav_md_preview', () => {
    expect(getNodeNavigationCommand('/notes/foo.md', true)).toBe(
      'markdown.showPreview'
    );
  });

  it('test_graph_view_get_node_nav_md_no_preview', () => {
    expect(getNodeNavigationCommand('/notes/foo.md', false)).toBe('vscode.open');
  });

  it('test_graph_view_get_node_nav_non_md', () => {
    expect(getNodeNavigationCommand('/notes/foo.txt', true)).toBe('vscode.open');
  });

  it('test_graph_view_merge_styles_patch_wins', () => {
    const merged = mergeStyles(
      { colorMode: 'none' },
      { colorMode: 'directory' }
    );
    expect(merged.colorMode).toBe('directory');
  });

  it('test_graph_view_merge_styles_base_preserved', () => {
    const merged = mergeStyles(
      { showNodesOfType: { tag: true, placeholder: false } },
      { colorMode: 'type' }
    );
    expect(merged.showNodesOfType).toEqual({ tag: true, placeholder: false });
  });

  it('test_graph_view_view_config_colors', () => {
    expect(
      viewConfigToStyle({ show: { tag: { color: '#ff0000' } } })
    ).toEqual({
      showNodesOfType: { tag: true },
      style: { node: { tag: '#ff0000' } },
    });
  });

  it('test_graph_view_view_config_visibility', () => {
    expect(
      viewConfigToStyle({
        show: { tag: { enabled: false }, placeholder: { enabled: true } },
      })
    ).toEqual({
      showNodesOfType: { tag: false, placeholder: true },
    });
  });

  it('test_graph_view_view_config_empty', () => {
    expect(viewConfigToStyle({})).toEqual({});
  });

  it('test_graph_view_get_graph_style_reads_config', async () => {
    await vscode.workspace
      .getConfiguration()
      .update('pa-hub.graph.style', { background: '#123456' });

    expect(getGraphStyle()).toEqual({
      style: { background: '#123456' },
    });
  });

  it('test_graph_view_cut_title_truncates', async () => {
    await vscode.workspace.getConfiguration().update('pa-hub.graph.titleMaxLength', 5);

    expect(cutTitle('Long title')).toBe('Long ...');
  });

  it('test_graph_view_cut_title_short_passthrough', async () => {
    await vscode.workspace.getConfiguration().update('pa-hub.graph.titleMaxLength', 20);

    expect(cutTitle('Short title')).toBe('Short title');
  });

  it('test_graph_view_resolve_view_no_args', () => {
    expect(resolveViewStyle()).toEqual({
      style: { style: {} },
      view: undefined,
    });
  });

  it('test_graph_view_handle_active_resource_no_args', () => {
    expect(() =>
      handleActiveResourceChange(undefined, {} as any, URI.file('/note.md'))
    ).not.toThrow();
  });
});

/**
 * Shared message types between the extension host and the graph webview.
 * This file must remain free of VS Code and Node.js imports.
 */

export const PA_HUB_PROTOCOL_VERSION = 1 as const;

export const ROOT_KINDS = ['vault', 'sources'] as const;
export type RootKind = (typeof ROOT_KINDS)[number];

export type NodeId = `${RootKind}:${string}`;

export type NodeType =
  | 'note'
  | 'tag'
  | 'placeholder'
  | 'image'
  | 'attachment'
  | string;

export interface NodeInfo {
  id: string;
  type: NodeType;
  title: string;
  properties: { color?: string; [key: string]: unknown };
  tags: Array<{ label: string }>;
  root?: RootKind;
}

export interface GraphData {
  nodeInfo: Record<string, NodeInfo>;
  links: Array<{ source: string; target: string }>;
  rootGroups?: RootGroup[];
  protocolVersion?: number;
}

export interface RootGroup {
  root: RootKind;
  label: string;
  color: string;
}

export interface StyleConfig {
  background?: string;
  fontSize?: number;
  fontFamily?: string;
  lineColor?: string;
  lineWidth?: number;
  particleWidth?: number;
  highlightedForeground?: string;
  node?: {
    note?: string;
    placeholder?: string;
    tag?: string;
    [key: string]: string | undefined;
  };
}

export type GroupMatchProperty = 'type' | 'path' | 'tag' | 'title' | string;

export interface GroupMatch {
  property: GroupMatchProperty;
  value: string;
}

export interface GroupRule {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  color: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  match: GroupMatch;
}

export interface GraphStyle {
  style?: StyleConfig;
  colorMode?: 'none' | 'directory' | 'type';
  groups?: GroupRule[];
  showNodesOfType?: Record<string, boolean>;
}

/** Config for a built-in special type (tag, attachment, image, placeholder) */
export interface BuiltinTypeConfig {
  enabled?: boolean;
  color?: string;
}

/**
 * A named, pre-configured graph view.
 * Also used as raw command args for `pa-hub.show-graph`.
 * Merge order: pa-hub.graph.style → named view → inline config.
 */
export interface GraphViewConfig {
  name?: string;
  colorBy?: 'none' | 'directory' | 'type';
  groups?: GroupRule[];
  /** Visibility and color for built-in types: tag, attachment, image, placeholder */
  show?: Record<string, BuiltinTypeConfig>;
  background?: string;
  fontSize?: number;
  fontFamily?: string;
  lineColor?: string;
}

export interface ShowGraphArgs {
  view?: string;
  config?: GraphViewConfig;
}

export interface FocusRequest {
  requestId: string;
  nodeId: NodeId;
  center?: boolean;
}

export interface FocusResult {
  requestId: string;
  nodeId: NodeId;
  ok: boolean;
  reason?: string;
}

// Extension → Webview
export type ExtensionMessage =
  | { type: 'didUpdateStyle'; payload: GraphStyle }
  | { type: 'didUpdateGraphData'; payload: GraphData }
  | { type: 'didSelectNote'; payload: string }
  | { type: 'didRequestFocus'; payload: FocusRequest }
  | { type: 'didChangeVisibility'; payload: { visible: boolean } };

// Webview → Extension
export type WebviewMessage =
  | { type: 'webviewDidLoad' }
  | { type: 'webviewDidSelectNode'; payload: string }
  | {
      type: 'error';
      payload: {
        message: string;
        filename: string;
        lineno: number;
        colno: number;
        error?: unknown;
      };
    }
  | { type: 'webviewDidBecomeReady'; payload: { protocolVersion: number } }
  | { type: 'webviewDidFocusNode'; payload: FocusResult };

export const EXTENSION_MESSAGE_TYPES = [
  'didUpdateStyle',
  'didUpdateGraphData',
  'didSelectNote',
  'didRequestFocus',
  'didChangeVisibility',
] as const;

export const WEBVIEW_MESSAGE_TYPES = [
  'webviewDidLoad',
  'webviewDidSelectNode',
  'error',
  'webviewDidBecomeReady',
  'webviewDidFocusNode',
] as const;

export type ParseResult<T> =
  | { ok: true; value: T }
  | { ok: false; reason: string };

export interface ParseOptions {
  maxChars?: number;
}

export const MAX_MESSAGE_CHARS = 2_000_000;

function isRootKind(value: unknown): value is RootKind {
  return value === 'vault' || value === 'sources';
}

function isValidNodePath(path: string): boolean {
  if (path.length === 0 || path.length > 1024 || path.startsWith('/')) {
    return false;
  }

  if (path.includes('\\') || path.includes('://')) {
    return false;
  }

  if (/^[A-Za-z]+:/.test(path) || /%2e|%2f|%5c/i.test(path)) {
    return false;
  }

  for (const character of path) {
    const codePoint = character.codePointAt(0);
    if (codePoint !== undefined && (codePoint < 0x20 || codePoint === 0x7f)) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return path
    .split('/')
    .every((segment) => segment !== '' && segment !== '.' && segment !== '..');
}

export function parseNodeId(value: unknown): { root: RootKind; path: string } | null {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > 1100) {
    return null;
  }

  const separator = value.indexOf(':');
  if (separator <= 0) {
    return null;
  }

  const root = value.slice(0, separator);
  const path = value.slice(separator + 1);
  if (!isRootKind(root) || !isValidNodePath(path)) {
    return null;
  }

  return { root, path };
}

export function isNodeId(value: unknown): value is NodeId {
  return parseNodeId(value) !== null;
}

export function makeNodeId(root: RootKind, path: string): NodeId {
  const value = `${root}:${path}`;
  if (!isNodeId(value)) {
    throw new RangeError('Invalid node id');
  }
  return value;
}

type MessageRecord = Record<string, unknown>;

interface MessageEnvelope {
  type: string;
  hasPayload: boolean;
  payload: unknown;
}

function isPlainObject(value: unknown): value is MessageRecord {
  return (
    typeof value === 'object' &&
    value !== null &&
    Object.getPrototypeOf(value) === Object.prototype
  );
}

function hasOwn(value: MessageRecord, key: string): boolean {
  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(value, key);
}

function hasForbiddenKey(value: MessageRecord): boolean {
  return Object.getOwnPropertyNames(value).some(
    (key) => key === '__proto__' || key === 'constructor' || key === 'prototype',
  );
}

function hasExtraKey(value: MessageRecord, allowed: readonly string[]): boolean {
  return Reflect.ownKeys(value).some(

---
name: backprop
description: |
  Bug → spec protocol. When a bug is found or a test fails, trace the cause,
  decide whether a new §V invariant would catch recurrence, append to §B.
  This is the one non-obvious thing SDD does that plan-then-execute doesn't.
  Triggers on test failure, bug report, post-mortem, or explicit user ask.
---

# backprop — bug → spec

Plan-then-execute fixes the code & forgets.
SDD fixes the code AND edits spec so recurrence is impossible.
That edit is backprop.

## WHEN TO BACKPROP

- Test failed at `/build` verification.
- User reports bug.
- Post-mortem after production incident.
- `/check` flags VIOLATE with root cause found.

## SIX STEPS

### 1. TRACE
Read failure output / bug report.
Find exact file:line of wrong behavior.
Name root cause in one caveman sentence.

### 2. ANALYZE
Ask three questions:
- Would a new §V invariant catch this class of bug? (most common: yes)
- Is §I wrong — did spec claim shape the code cannot deliver? (sometimes)
- Is §T wrong — did we build the wrong thing? (rare but real)

### 3. PROPOSE
Draft the spec change. Never skip §B; §V/§I/§T are case-by-case.

Template:
```
§B row: B<next>|<date>|<root cause>|V<N>
§V line: V<next>: <testable rule that would have caught it>
```

Example:
```
§B row: B3|2026-04-20|refund job ran twice on retry|V7
§V line: V7: ∀ refund → idempotency key check before charge reversal
```

### 4. GENERATE TEST
New invariant without test = lie. Add failing test first.
Name test so it cites the invariant: `TestV7_RefundIdempotent`.

### 5. VERIFY
Fix code. Run test. Must pass. Run full suite. Must not regress.

### 6. LOG
Commit spec edit + test + code fix together.
Commit msg: `backprop §B.<n> + §V.<N>: <one-line cause>`.

## WHAT MAKES A GOOD INVARIANT

- Testable in code (grep-able or assert-able).
- Scoped to a behavior, not a file.
- Stated positively when possible (`! hold` over `⊥ forbid`).
- References §I surface where it applies.

**Bad**: V8: code should be correct.
**Good**: V8: ∀ pg_query ! params interpolated via driver, ⊥ string concat.

## WHEN NOT TO ADD §V

- Bug was purely mechanical typo with no class (`i++` vs `i--` in throwaway).
- Fix is a one-time migration.
- Root cause is external dep (upgrade deps instead, note in §C).

Still append §B entry — record that this failure mode was considered. Future bug with same smell → §B search shows precedent.

## OUTPUT SHAPE

Every backprop run produces:
1. §B entry (always).
2. §V entry (usually).
3. Test file (when §V added).
4. Code fix.
5. One commit.

No dashboards. No log files. SPEC.md + git is the full history.

+  private updateGraph(webviewHost: GraphWebviewHost, foam: Foam): void {
+    const { graphData, idToUri } = buildRootQualifiedGraphData(
+      foam.workspace.list(),
+      foam.graph.getAllConnections(),

+    }
+

import * as vscode from 'vscode';

  getGraphStyle,
  getNodeNavigationCommand,
  handleActiveResourceChange,
  mergeStyles,
  viewConfigToStyle,
  resolveViewStyle,
} from '.';

describe('handleActiveResourceChange', () => {
  const makePanel = () =>
    ({
      webview: { postMessage: vi.fn() },
    } as unknown as vscode.WebviewPanel);

  const makeWorkspace = () => new FoamWorkspace([URI.file('/')]);

  it('does not throw when panel is undefined', () => {
    const foam = { workspace: makeWorkspace() } as any;
    expect(() =>
      handleActiveResourceChange(undefined, foam, URI.file('/some/note.md'))
    ).not.toThrow();
  });

  it('does not throw when uri is undefined', () => {
    const panel = makePanel();
    const foam = { workspace: makeWorkspace() } as any;
    expect(() =>
      handleActiveResourceChange(panel, foam, undefined)
    ).not.toThrow();
    expect(panel.webview.postMessage).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('does not post message for untitled documents', () => {
    const panel = makePanel();
    const foam = { workspace: makeWorkspace() } as any;
    const uri = new URI({ scheme: 'untitled', path: 'untitled-1' });
    expect(() => handleActiveResourceChange(panel, foam, uri)).not.toThrow();
    expect(panel.webview.postMessage).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('does not post message nor throw for files not in the workspace', () => {
    const panel = makePanel();
    const foam = { workspace: makeWorkspace() } as any;
    expect(() =>
      handleActiveResourceChange(panel, foam, URI.file('/not/a/note.json'))
    ).not.toThrow();
    expect(panel.webview.postMessage).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('posts a didSelectNote message for notes in the workspace', () => {
    const panel = makePanel();
    const ws = createTestWorkspace();
    const note = createTestNote({ uri: '/notes/note-a.md' });
    ws.set(note);
    const foam = { workspace: ws } as any;
    handleActiveResourceChange(panel, foam, note.uri);
    expect(panel.webview.postMessage).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      type: 'didSelectNote',
      payload: note.uri.path,
    });
  });
});

  describe('when navigateToPreview is false', () => {

    it('returns vscode.open for non-markdown files', () => {

      );

      );

    it('returns vscode.open for files with no extension', () => {

 succeeded in 0ms:
import * as vscode from 'vscode';
import { Foam, URI } from '@foam/core';
import { Logger } from '@foam/core';
import { fromVsCodeUri, toVsCodeUri } from '../../utils/vsc-utils';
import { isSome } from '@foam/core';
import { getFoamVsCodeConfig } from '../../config';
import { getActiveTabUri, onDidChangeActiveTab } from '../../services/editor';
import { buildRootQualifiedGraphData } from '../../../pa-hub/graph-data';
import {
  buildCsp,
  fillGraphHtml,
  generateNonce,
} from '../../../pa-hub/webview-security';
import type {
  GraphStyle,
  GraphViewConfig,
  ShowGraphArgs,
} from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with { 'resolution-mode': 'import' };
import type { NodeId } from '@foam/graph-view/protocol' with {
  'resolution-mode': 'import',
};

interface GraphWebviewHost {
  webview: vscode.Webview;
}

class GraphWebviewProvider implements vscode.WebviewViewProvider {
  private webviewView: vscode.WebviewView | undefined;
  private idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI> = new Map();

  constructor(
    private readonly context: vscode.ExtensionContext,
    private readonly foamPromise: Promise<Foam>
  ) {}

  async resolveWebviewView(
    webviewView: vscode.WebviewView,
    _context: vscode.WebviewViewResolveContext,
    _token: vscode.CancellationToken
  ): Promise<void> {
    this.webviewView = webviewView;

    const datavizUri = vscode.Uri.joinPath(
      this.context.extensionUri,
      'static',
      'dataviz'
    );
    webviewView.webview.options = {
      enableScripts: true,
      localResourceRoots: [datavizUri],
    };

    const nonce = generateNonce();
    const csp = buildCsp({
      cspSource: webviewView.webview.cspSource,
      nonce,
    });
    const template = new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(
      await vscode.workspace.fs.readFile(
        vscode.Uri.joinPath(datavizUri, 'index.html')
      )
    );
    const filled = fillGraphHtml(template, {
      csp,
      nonce,
      resolveAsset: fileName =>
        webviewView.webview
          .asWebviewUri(vscode.Uri.joinPath(datavizUri, fileName))
          .toString(),
    });
    webviewView.webview.html = filled;

    const foam = await this.foamPromise;
    webviewView.webview.onDidReceiveMessage(
      async message => {
        switch (message.type) {
          case 'webviewDidLoad': {
            const style = getGraphStyle();
            webviewView.webview.postMessage({
              type: 'didUpdateStyle',
              payload: style,
            });

            this.updateGraph(webviewView, foam);
            break;
          }
          case 'webviewDidSelectNode': {
            const resolvedUri = this.idToUri.get(message.payload as NodeId);
            if (!resolvedUri) {
              break;
            }

            const noteUri = toVsCodeUri(resolvedUri);
            const selectedNote = foam.workspace.get(resolvedUri);

            if (isSome(selectedNote)) {
              const navigateToPreview = getFoamVsCodeConfig(
                'graph.navigateToPreview',
                false
              );
              const command = getNodeNavigationCommand(
                resolvedUri.path,
                navigateToPreview
              );
              if (command === 'markdown.showPreview') {
                vscode.commands.executeCommand(command, noteUri);
              } else {
                vscode.commands.executeCommand(
                  command,
                  noteUri,
                  vscode.ViewColumn.One
                );
              }
            }
            break;
          }
          case 'webviewDidBecomeReady': {
            break;
          }
          case 'error': {
            Logger.error('An error occurred in the graph view', message.payload);
            break;
          }
        }
      },
      undefined,
      this.context.subscriptions
    );

    const noteUpdatedListener = foam.graph.onDidUpdate(() => {
      this.updateGraph(webviewView, foam);
    });
    const editorListener = onDidChangeActiveTab(() => {
      handleActiveResourceChange(
        webviewView,
        foam,
        getActiveTabUri(foam.workspace),
        this.idToUri
      );
    });
    webviewView.onDidDispose(() => {
      noteUpdatedListener.dispose();
      editorListener.dispose();
      if (this.webviewView === webviewView) {
        this.webviewView = undefined;
      }
    });
  }

  updateStyle(style: GraphStyle): void {
    this.webviewView?.webview.postMessage({
      type: 'didUpdateStyle',
      payload: style,
    });
  }

  private updateGraph(webviewHost: GraphWebviewHost, foam: Foam): void {
    const { graphData, idToUri } = buildRootQualifiedGraphData(
      foam.workspace.list(),
      foam.graph.getAllConnections(),
      vscode.workspace.workspaceFolders?.map(folder => fromVsCodeUri(folder.uri)) ?? []
    );
    this.idToUri = idToUri;

    const maxLen = vscode.workspace
      .getConfiguration('pa-hub.graph')
      .get<number>('titleMaxLength', 24);
    for (const node of Object.values(graphData.nodeInfo)) {
      node.title = cutTitle(node.title, maxLen);
    }

    webviewHost.webview.postMessage({
      type: 'didUpdateGraphData',
      payload: graphData,
    });
  }
}

export default async function activate(
  context: vscode.ExtensionContext,
  foamPromise: Promise<Foam>
) {
  const provider = new GraphWebviewProvider(context, foamPromise);
  context.subscriptions.push(
    vscode.workspace.onDidChangeConfiguration(event => {
      if (event.affectsConfiguration('pa-hub.graph.style')) {
        provider.updateStyle(getGraphStyle());
      }
    }),
    vscode.window.registerWebviewViewProvider('pa-hub.graph', provider, {
      webviewOptions: { retainContextWhenHidden: true },
    })
  );

  context.subscriptions.push(
    vscode.commands.registerCommand(
      'pa-hub.show-graph',
      () => vscode.commands.executeCommand('pa-hub.graph.focus')
    )
  );
}

export function cutTitle(title: string, maxLen: number): string {
  if (maxLen > 0 && title.length > maxLen) {
    return title.substring(0, maxLen).concat('...');
  }
  return title;
}

export function getGraphStyle(): GraphStyle {
  const styleConfig =
    vscode.workspace.getConfiguration('pa-hub.graph').get('style') ?? {};
  return { style: styleConfig };
}

export function viewConfigToStyle(config: GraphViewConfig): GraphStyle {
  const nodeColors = config.show
    ? Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(config.show)
          .filter(([, cfg]) => cfg.color)
          .map(([type, cfg]) => [type, cfg.color!])
      )
    : undefined;

  const showNodesOfType = config.show
    ? Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(config.show).map(([type, cfg]) => [
          type,
          cfg.enabled ?? true,
        ])
      )
    : undefined;

  const styleProps: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (config.background !== undefined)
    styleProps.background = config.background;
  if (config.fontSize !== undefined) styleProps.fontSize = config.fontSize;
  if (config.fontFamily !== undefined)
    styleProps.fontFamily = config.fontFamily;
  if (config.lineColor !== undefined) styleProps.lineColor = config.lineColor;
  if (nodeColors && Object.keys(nodeColors).length > 0)
    styleProps.node = nodeColors;

  return {
    ...(config.colorBy !== undefined ? { colorMode: config.colorBy } : {}),
    ...(config.groups !== undefined ? { groups: config.groups } : {}),
    ...(showNodesOfType !== undefined ? { showNodesOfType } : {}),
    ...(Object.keys(styleProps).length > 0 ? { style: styleProps } : {}),
  };
}

export function mergeStyles(base: GraphStyle, patch: GraphStyle): GraphStyle {
  return {
    colorMode: patch.colorMode ?? base.colorMode,
    groups: patch.groups ?? base.groups,
    showNodesOfType: { ...base.showNodesOfType, ...patch.showNodesOfType },
    style: { ...base.style, ...patch.style },
  };
}

export function resolveViewStyle(args?: ShowGraphArgs): {
  style: GraphStyle;
  view?: string;
} {
  const views: GraphViewConfig[] =
    vscode.workspace.getConfiguration('pa-hub.graph').get('views') ?? [];

  let style: GraphStyle = getGraphStyle();

  // If no view name given and no inline config, fall back to the "Default" named view
  const namedView = args?.view ?? (args?.config ? undefined : 'Default');

  if (namedView) {
    const view = views.find(v => v.name === namedView);
    if (view) {
      style = mergeStyles(style, viewConfigToStyle(view));
    }
  }

  if (args?.config) {
    style = mergeStyles(style, viewConfigToStyle(args.config));
  }

  return { style, view: args?.view };
}

export function handleActiveResourceChange(
  webviewHost: GraphWebviewHost | undefined,
  foam: Foam,
  uri: URI | undefined,
  idToUri: Map<NodeId, URI> = new Map()
) {
  if (webviewHost && uri && uri.scheme !== 'untitled') {
    const note = foam.workspace.find(uri);
    if (isSome(note)) {
      let nodeId: NodeId | undefined;
      for (const [id, resourceUri] of idToUri) {
        if (resourceUri.isEqual(uri)) {
          nodeId = id;
          break;
        }
      }

      if (!nodeId) {
        return;
      }

      webviewHost.webview.postMessage({
        type: 'didSelectNote',
        payload: nodeId,
      });
    }
  }
}

export function getNodeNavigationCommand(
  uriPath: string,
  navigateToPreview: boolean
): string {
  if (navigateToPreview && uriPath.endsWith('.md')) {
    return 'markdown.showPreview';
  }
  return 'vscode.open';
}

+
+  private updateGraph(webviewHost: GraphWebviewHost, foam: Foam): void {
+    const { graphData, idToUri } = buildRootQualifiedGraphData(
+      foam.workspace.list(),
+      foam.graph.getAllConnections(),
+      vscode.workspace.workspaceFolders?.map(folder => fromVsCodeUri(folder.uri)) ?? []
+    );
+    this.idToUri = idToUri;
+
+    const maxLen = vscode.workspace
+      .getConfiguration('pa-hub.graph')
+      .get<number>('titleMaxLength', 24);
+    for (const node of Object.values(graphData.nodeInfo)) {
+      node.title = cutTitle(node.title, maxLen);
+    }
+
+    webviewHost.webview.postMessage({
+      type: 'didUpdateGraphData',
+      payload: graphData,
+    });
+  }
 }
